\end{pmatrix}

\mathbf{6_{2}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{5}\beta_{3}-2\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+2\alpha_{4}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+2\alpha_{5}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\alpha_{4}\beta_{6}+2\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}+2\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}\\
2\alpha_{5}\beta_{6}-2\alpha_{4}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}\\
\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{5}\beta_{2}+2\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}\\
\end{pmatrix}

\mathbf{6_{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{5}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{4}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{4}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{5}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{5}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{5}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{4}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{5}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}\\
\end{pmatrix}

\mathbf{6}\otimes\mathbf{6}=\mathbf{1_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{3_{1,s}}\oplus\mathbf{3_{2,s}}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{1,s}}\oplus\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{2,s}}\oplus\mathbf{4_{s}}\oplus\mathbf{4_{a}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{1,s}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{2,a}}\oplus\mathbf{5_{3,a}}

\mathbf{1_{a}}=\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{6}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}+\alpha_{5}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{5}

\mathbf{3_{1,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{6}\beta_{3}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{5}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{5}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{5}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}\\
\end{pmatrix}

\mathbf{3_{2,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}2\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}+2\alpha_{5}\beta_{4}+2\alpha_{4}\beta_{5}-2\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-2\alpha_{6}\beta_{4}-2\alpha_{4}\beta_{6}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}-2\alpha_{5}\beta_{3}-2\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}

\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{1,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{6}\beta_{3}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}\\
\alpha_{6}\beta_{5}+\alpha_{5}\beta_{6}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
-\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}\\
\end{pmatrix}

\mathbf{3^{\prime}_{2,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{6}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}-\alpha_{5}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{5}\\
\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{6}+\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}+\alpha_{5}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}

\mathbf{4_{s}}=\begin{pmatrix}2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}+2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}+\alpha_{6}\beta_{4}+\alpha_{4}\beta_{6}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{5}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}\\
2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}-3\alpha_{4}\beta_{1}-3\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{6}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{5}\beta_{6}\\
2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{6}+\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-3\alpha_{5}\beta_{2}-3\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}+2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{2}+2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{5}\beta_{3}-\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}

\mathbf{4_{a}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{4}\beta_{6}-\alpha_{6}\beta_{4}\\
\alpha_{4}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{6}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{5}\beta_{6}\\
\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}+\alpha_{5}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}-\alpha_{4}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}-\alpha_{5}\beta_{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}

\mathbf{5_{1,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{2}\\
2\alpha_{5}\beta_{5}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}+\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{6}\\
2\alpha_{3}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{5}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{5}\beta_{6}\\
2\alpha_{6}\beta_{6}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}\\
2\alpha_{4}\beta_{4}+\alpha_{6}\beta_{2}+\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}-\alpha_{6}\beta_{1}-\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{5}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}

\mathbf{5_{2,a}}=\begin{pmatrix}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}-\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}+2\alpha_{5}\beta_{4}-2\alpha_{4}\beta_{5}+\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}-\alpha_{6}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{6}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{5}\beta_{6}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{4}\beta_{3}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{6}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}

\mathbf{5_{3,a}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{2}\alpha_{2}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{1}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{2}\alpha_{5}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{2}\alpha_{4}\beta_{5}+2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{3}\beta_{6}-2\sqrt{2}\alpha_{6}\beta_{3}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{1}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{3}\beta_{2}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{6}\beta_{4}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{4}\beta_{6}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{4}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{4}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{4}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{4}\\
\sqrt{6}\alpha_{5}\beta_{2}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{2}\beta_{5}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{1}\beta_{5}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{5}\beta_{1}\\
\sqrt{3}\alpha_{6}\beta_{1}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{1}\beta_{6}+\sqrt{6}\alpha_{5}\beta_{3}-\sqrt{6}\alpha_{3}\beta_{5}+\sqrt{3}\alpha_{2}\beta_{6}-\sqrt{3}\alpha_{6}\beta_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}

Appendix B
Construct weight 1 and level 5 modular forms with Klein form

The linear space
\mathcal{M}_{k}(\Gamma(5))
of modular forms of positive integral weight
k
and level 5 has been explicitly constructed through the Dedekind eta-function and Klein form as follow
[
85
]

\mathcal{M}_{k}(\Gamma(5))=\bigoplus_{a+b=5k,\,a,b\geq 0}\mathbb{C}\frac{\eta^{15k}(5\tau)}{\eta^{3k}(\tau)}\mathfrak{k}^{a}_{\frac{1}{5},\frac{0}{5}}(5\tau)\mathfrak{k}^{b}_{\frac{2}{5},\frac{0}{5}}(5\tau)\,,

(B.1)

where the expression of the eta-function
\eta(\tau)
is
[
86
,
87
,
88
]
,

\eta(\tau)=q^{1/24}\prod_{n=1}^{\infty}\left(1-q^{n}\right),\qquad q\equiv e^{i2\pi\tau}\,.

(B.2)

The Klein form
\mathfrak{k}_{(r_{1},r_{2})}(\tau)
in Eq. (
B.1
) is a holomorphic function which has no zeros and poles on the upper half complex plane. The Klein form can be written into an infinite product expansion
[
89
,
90
,
88
,
91
]
:

\mathfrak{k}_{(r_{1},r_{2})}(\tau)=q^{(r_{1}-1)/2}_{z}(1-q_{z})\prod_{n=1}^{\infty}(1-q^{n}q_{z})(1-q^{n}q_{z}^{-1})(1-q^{n})^{-2}\,,

(B.3)

where
q_{z}=e^{2\pi iz}
with
z=r_{1}\tau+r_{2}
.
From Eq. (
B.1
) we see that the modular forms of weight
k
and level 5 spans a linear space of dimension
5k+1
. For the weight
k=1
modular forms, the modular space
\mathcal{M}_{1}(\Gamma(5))
has dimension 6 and the basis vectors can be chosen to be

\displaystyle e_{1}(\tau)=\frac{\eta^{15}(5\tau)}{\eta^{3}(\tau)}\mathfrak{k}^{5}_{\frac{2}{5},0}(5\tau),~~~~~~~~~~\quad\qquad e_{2}(\tau)=\frac{\eta^{15}(5\tau)}{\eta^{3}(\tau)}\mathfrak{k}_{\frac{1}{5},0}(5\tau)\mathfrak{k}^{4}_{\frac{2}{5},0}(5\tau),

\displaystyle e_{3}(\tau)=\frac{\eta^{15}(5\tau)}{\eta^{3}(\tau)}\mathfrak{k}^{2}_{\frac{1}{5},0}(5\tau)\mathfrak{k}^{3}_{\frac{2}{5},0}(5\tau),\quad\qquad e_{4}(\tau)=\frac{\eta^{15}(5\tau)}{\eta^{3}(\tau)}\mathfrak{k}^{3}_{\frac{1}{5},0}(5\tau)\mathfrak{k}^{2}_{\frac{2}{5},0}(5\tau),

\displaystyle e_{5}(\tau)=\frac{\eta^{15}(5\tau)}{\eta^{3}(\tau)}\mathfrak{k}^{4}_{\frac{1}{5},0}(5\tau)\mathfrak{k}_{\frac{2}{5},0}(5\tau),\quad\qquad e_{6}(\tau)=\frac{\eta^{15}(5\tau)}{\eta^{3}(\tau)}\mathfrak{k}^{5}_{\frac{1}{5},0}(5\tau)\,.

(B.4)

The above basis vectors are linearly independent and they span the weight 1 modular space
\mathcal{M}_{1}(\Gamma(5))
. Any modular function of weight 1 and level 5 can be expressed as a linear combination of the basis elements
e_{i}
with
i=1,2,\ldots,6
. Under the action of the generator
T
, they transform as

\displaystyle e_{1}(\tau)\rightarrow e_{1}(\tau),\qquad e_{2}(\tau)\rightarrow e^{i\frac{2\pi}{5}}e_{2}(\tau),\qquad e_{3}(\tau)\rightarrow e^{i\frac{4\pi}{5}}e_{3}(\tau),

(B.5)

\displaystyle e_{4}(\tau)\rightarrow e^{i\frac{6\pi}{5}}e_{4}(\tau),\qquad e_{5}(\tau)\rightarrow e^{i\frac{8\pi}{5}}e_{5}(\tau),\qquad e_{6}(\tau)\rightarrow e_{6}(\tau)\,.

Furthermore, we find the following transformation properties under another generator
S
,

\displaystyle e_{1}(\tau)

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle-\tau\frac{\sqrt{5}i}{\sqrt{5\sqrt{5}\phi}}\Bigg\{\frac{\phi^{3}}{5}e_{1}(\tau)+\phi^{2}e_{2}(\tau)+2\phi e_{3}(\tau)+2e_{4}(\tau)+\frac{1}{\phi}e_{5}(\tau)+\frac{1}{5\phi^{2}}e_{6}(\tau)\Bigg\}\,,

\displaystyle e_{2}(\tau)

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle-\tau\frac{i}{\sqrt{5\sqrt{5}\phi}}\Bigg\{\frac{\phi^{2}}{\sqrt{5}}e_{1}(\tau)+e_{2}(\tau)-2e_{3}(\tau)-2\phi e_{4}(\tau)-\phi e_{5}(\tau)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{5}\phi}e_{6}(\tau)\Bigg\}\,,

\displaystyle e_{3}(\tau)

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle-\tau\frac{i}{\sqrt{5\sqrt{5}\phi}}\Bigg\{\frac{\phi}{\sqrt{5}}e_{1}(\tau)-e_{2}(\tau)-\phi e_{3}(\tau)+e_{4}(\tau)+\phi e_{5}(\tau)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}e_{6}(\tau)\Bigg\}\,,

\displaystyle e_{4}(\tau)

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle-\tau\frac{i}{\sqrt{5\sqrt{5}\phi}}\Bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{5}}e_{1}(\tau)-\phi e_{2}(\tau)+e_{3}(\tau)+\phi e_{4}(\tau)-e_{5}(\tau)-\frac{\phi}{\sqrt{5}}e_{6}(\tau)\Bigg\}\,,

\displaystyle e_{5}(\tau)

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle-\tau\frac{i}{\sqrt{5\sqrt{5}\phi}}\Bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{5}\phi}e_{1}(\tau)-\phi e_{2}(\tau)+2\phi e_{3}(\tau)-2e_{4}(\tau)-e_{5}(\tau)+\frac{\phi^{2}}{\sqrt{5}}e_{6}(\tau)\Bigg\}\,,

\displaystyle e_{6}(\tau)

\displaystyle\rightarrow

\displaystyle-\tau\frac{\sqrt{5}i}{\sqrt{5\sqrt{5}\phi}}\Bigg\{\frac{1}{5\phi^{2}}e_{1}(\tau)-\frac{1}{\phi}e_{2}(\tau)+2e_{3}(\tau)-2\phi e_{4}(\tau)+\phi^{2}e_{5}(\tau)-\frac{\phi^{3}}{5}e_{6}(\tau)\Bigg\}\,,

(B.6)

where
\phi=(1+\sqrt{5})/2
is the golden ratio. We see that the basis vectors
e_{i}
are closed under
S
and
T
up to multiplicative factors, and each element is exactly mapped into itself under the action of
S^{4}
,
(ST)^{3}
and
T^{5}
. Therefore we conclude that
e_{i}
really span the whole modular space
\mathcal{M}_{1}(\Gamma(5))
. As shown in
[
9
,
53
]
, the modular form space of weight
k
and level
N
can always be decomposed into different irreducible representations of
\Gamma^{\prime}_{N}
. We can perform linear combinations of the six basis vectors
e_{1,2,3,4,5,6}
to form a six dimensional representation
\mathbf{6}
of
A^{\prime}_{5}
,

Y_{\mathbf{6}}(\tau)=\left(Y_{1}(\tau),Y_{2}(\tau),Y_{3}(\tau),Y_{4}(\tau),Y_{5}(\tau),Y_{6}(\tau)\right)^{T}\,,

(B.7)

with

\displaystyle Y_{1}(\tau)=e_{1}(\tau)+2e_{6}(\tau),\quad Y_{2}(\tau)=2e_{1}(\tau)-e_{6}(\tau),\quad Y_{3}(\tau)=5e_{2}(\tau),

\displaystyle Y_{4}(\tau)=5\sqrt{2}e_{3}(\tau),\quad Y_{5}(\tau)=-5\sqrt{2}e_{4}(\tau),\quad Y_{6}(\tau)=5e_{5}(\tau)\,.

(B.8)

Under the actions of the modular symmetry generators
S
and
T
, the modular form
Y_{\mathbf{6}}(\tau)
transforms as

Y_{\mathbf{6}}(-1/\tau)=-\tau\rho_{\mathbf{6}}(S)Y_{\mathbf{6}}(\tau),\quad Y_{\mathbf{6}}(\tau+1)=\rho_{\mathbf{6}}(T)Y_{\mathbf{6}}(\tau)\,,

(B.9)

where the six dimensional representation matrices
\rho_{\mathbf{6}}(S)
and
\rho_{\mathbf{6}}(T)
are given in table
11
. Furthermore, we can read out the
q
-expansion of the modular forms
Y_{i}
as

\displaystyle Y_{1}(\tau)=1+5q+10q^{3}-5q^{4}+5q^{5}+10q^{6}+5q^{9}+\ldots\,,

\displaystyle Y_{2}(\tau)=2+5q+10q^{2}+5q^{4}+5q^{5}+10q^{6}+10q^{7}-5q^{9}+\ldots\,,

\displaystyle Y_{3}(\tau)=5q^{1/5}\left(1+2q+2q^{2}+q^{3}+2q^{4}+2q^{5}+2q^{6}+q^{7}+2q^{8}+2q^{9}+\ldots\right)\,,

\displaystyle Y_{4}(\tau)=5\sqrt{2}q^{2/5}\left(1+q+q^{2}+q^{3}+2q^{4}+q^{6}+q^{7}+2q^{8}+q^{9}+\ldots\right)\,,

\displaystyle Y_{5}(\tau)=-5\sqrt{2}q^{3/5}\left(1+q^{2}+q^{3}+q^{4}-q^{5}+2q^{6}+q^{8}+q^{9}+\ldots\right)\,,

\displaystyle Y_{6}(\tau)=5q^{4/5}\left(1-q+2q^{2}+2q^{6}-2q^{7}+2q^{8}+q^{9}+\ldots\right)\,.

(B.10)

Appendix C
Prove the identities in Eq. (
100
)

In section
6
, we find the identities between the theta constants and Kelin forms in Eq. (
100
) which is checked numerically. We will prove them by using the famous Jacobi triple product identity. Given the definition of eta function, Klein form and theta constant in Eqs. (
B.2
,
B.3
,
93
), the identities in Eq. (
100
) become the following formulas,

\displaystyle\sum_{n=-\infty}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}q^{(5n^{2}+n)/2}=(1-q^{2})\prod^{\infty}_{n=1}(1-q^{5n})(1-q^{5n-2})(1-q^{5n+2})\,,

(C.1)

\displaystyle\sum_{n=-\infty}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}q^{(5n^{2}+3n)/2}=(1-q)\prod^{\infty}_{n=1}(1-q^{5n})(1-q^{5n+1})(1-q^{5n-1})\,.

(C.2)

Notice that these formulas relate the infinite triple product with infinite summation, this reminds us of the famous Jacobi triple product identity which is the special case of the more general Macdonald identity
[
92
,
93
]
:

\sum_{n=-\infty}^{\infty}x^{n^{2}}y^{n}=\prod^{\infty}_{n=1}(1-x^{2n})(1+x^{2n-1}y)(1+x^{2n-1}y^{-1})\,,

(C.3)

where
x,y\in\mathbb{C}
and
|x|<1,\,y\neq 0
.
Taking
x=q^{5/2}
and
y=-q^{1/2}
in the above Jacobi triple product identity, we can straightforwardly obtain Eq. (
C.1
), and similarly Eq. (
C.2
) follows from
x=q^{5/2}
and
y=-q^{3/2}
.

Appendix D
Higher weight modular forms of level 5

The weight 4 modular multiplets can be generated from the product of
Y_{\mathbf{6}}
and the modular forms of weight 3. We find there are 21 linearly independent modular forms which can be taken to be

\begin{split}Y_{\mathbf{1}}^{(4)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{6}I}^{(3)})_{\mathbf{1}_{a}}=\begin{pmatrix}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,1}^{(3)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,2}^{(3)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,3}^{(3)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,4}^{(3)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,5}^{(3)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,6}^{(3)}\\
-\sqrt{2}\left(Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,2}^{(3)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,3}^{(3)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,5}^{(3)}\right)\\
-\sqrt{2}\left(Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,1}^{(3)}+Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,4}^{(3)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,6}^{(3)}\right)\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{3}}^{(4)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{6}II}^{(3)})_{\mathbf{3}_{1,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,2}^{(3)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,1}^{(3)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,6}^{(3)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,5}^{(3)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,4}^{(3)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,3}^{(3)}\\
-\sqrt{2}Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,3}^{(3)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,2}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,5}^{(3)}\\
-\sqrt{2}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,6}^{(3)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,4}^{(3)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,1}^{(3)}\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}}^{(4)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{6}I}^{(3)})_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}_{2,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
Y_{4}\left(Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}-Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}\right)-\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+\left(Y_{2}-Y_{1}\right)Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}\\
Y_{5}\left(Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}+Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}\right)+(Y_{1}+Y_{2})Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{4}}^{(4)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{6}I}^{(3)})_{\mathbf{4}_{a}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}+Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
Y_{4}\left(Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}-Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}\right)-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
Y_{5}\left(Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}+Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}\right)-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}\\
\sqrt{2}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{5}I}^{(4)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{6}I}^{(3)})_{\mathbf{5}_{1,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{6}\left(Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}\right)\\
Y_{3}\left(Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}+Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}\right)+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}+2Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}+2Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}\left(Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}+Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\right)\\
-\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}+2Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
Y_{6}\left(Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}-Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}\right)-\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+2Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{5}II}^{(4)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{6}I}^{(3)})_{\mathbf{5}_{2,a}}=\begin{pmatrix}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+2Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}-2Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\\
\sqrt{6}\left(Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}\right)\\
\sqrt{3}\left(Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}\right)\\
\sqrt{3}\left(-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(3)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(3)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(3)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(3)}\right)\\
\sqrt{6}\left(Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(3)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(3)}\right)\\
\end{pmatrix}\,.\end{split}

Using the Clebsch-Gordan coefficients listed in Appendix
A
, we can express each entry of these weight four modular forms as quartic polynomial of
Y_{i}
. In the same fashion, we can obtain the modular multiplets of weight 5 as follow,

\begin{split}Y_{\mathbf{2}}^{(5)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{5}II}^{(4)})_{\mathbf{2}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{3}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}-2Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+2Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\\
-\sqrt{3}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+2Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}+2Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{2^{\prime}}}^{(5)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{5}II}^{(4)})_{\mathbf{2^{\prime}}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{6}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}+3Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}-2Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+2\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\\
\sqrt{6}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}-2\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+2Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}+3Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{4^{\prime}}}^{(5)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{5}II}^{(4)})_{\mathbf{4^{\prime}}_{1}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{6}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}-2Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\\
\sqrt{3}\left(Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\right)\\
\sqrt{3}\left(\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+\left(Y_{1}-Y_{2}\right)Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}\right)\\
-\sqrt{6}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}-2Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{6}I}^{(5)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{1}}^{(4)}Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)})_{\mathbf{6}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{1},1}^{(4)}\\
Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{1},1}^{(4)}\\
Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{1},1}^{(4)}\\
Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{1},1}^{(4)}\\
Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{1},1}^{(4)}\\
Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{1},1}^{(4)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\quad\quad Y_{\mathbf{6}II}^{(5)}=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}}^{(4)})_{\mathbf{6}_{1}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},1}^{(4)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},3}^{(4)}\\
Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},2}^{(4)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},1}^{(4)}\\
Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},1}^{(4)}+Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},3}^{(4)}\\
Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},3}^{(4)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},2}^{(4)}\\
Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},2}^{(4)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},3}^{(4)}\\
Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},2}^{(4)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime},1}^{(4)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,\\
Y_{\mathbf{6}III}^{(5)}&=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{5}II}^{(4)})_{\mathbf{6}_{2}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{3}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}-2Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\\
\sqrt{3}Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+2Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}\\
-\sqrt{3}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}+2Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\\
2Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+2Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}\\
-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}-2Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,4}^{(4)}+2Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\\
-\sqrt{3}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,1}^{(4)}+2Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,2}^{(4)}+Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,3}^{(4)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{5}II,5}^{(4)}\end{pmatrix}\,.\end{split}

Finally we report the linearly independent weight 6 modular multiplets,

\displaystyle Y_{\mathbf{1}}^{(6)}

\displaystyle=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{6}II}^{(5)})_{\mathbf{1}_{a}}=Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,1}^{(5)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,2}^{(5)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,3}^{(5)}+Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,4}^{(5)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,5}^{(5)}-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,6}^{(5)}\,,

\displaystyle Y_{\mathbf{3}I}^{(6)}

\displaystyle=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{6}I}^{(5)})_{\mathbf{3}_{1,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(5)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(5)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(5)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(5)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(5)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(5)}\\
-\sqrt{2}\left(Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(5)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(5)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(5)}\right)\\
-\sqrt{2}\left(Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(5)}+Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(5)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(5)}\right)\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

\displaystyle Y_{\mathbf{3}II}^{(6)}

\displaystyle=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{6}III}^{(5)})_{\mathbf{3}_{1,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,1}^{(5)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,2}^{(5)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,3}^{(5)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,4}^{(5)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,5}^{(5)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,6}^{(5)}\\
-\sqrt{2}\left(Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,2}^{(5)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,3}^{(5)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,5}^{(5)}\right)\\
-\sqrt{2}\left(Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,1}^{(5)}+Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,4}^{(5)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,6}^{(5)}\right)\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

\displaystyle Y_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}I}^{(6)}

\displaystyle=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{6}I}^{(5)})_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}_{1,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(5)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(5)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(5)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(5)}\\
-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,2}^{(5)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(5)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(5)}+Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,6}^{(5)}\\
-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,1}^{(5)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,3}^{(5)}-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,4}^{(5)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}I,5}^{(5)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

\displaystyle Y_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}II}^{(6)}

\displaystyle=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{6}III}^{(5)})_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}_{1,s}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,1}^{(5)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,2}^{(5)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,3}^{(5)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,6}^{(5)}\\
-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,2}^{(5)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,4}^{(5)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,5}^{(5)}+Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,6}^{(5)}\\
-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,1}^{(5)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,3}^{(5)}-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,4}^{(5)}-Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}III,5}^{(5)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

\displaystyle Y_{\mathbf{4}I}^{(6)}

\displaystyle=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{2^{\prime}}}^{(5)})_{\mathbf{4}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{2}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{2}^{\prime},1}^{(5)}\\
\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{2}^{\prime},1}^{(5)}+Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{2}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}\\
Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{2}^{\prime},1}^{(5)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{2}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}\\
-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{2}^{\prime},1}^{(5)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{2}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

\displaystyle Y_{\mathbf{4}II}^{(6)}

\displaystyle=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{4^{\prime}}}^{(5)})_{\mathbf{4}_{1}}=\begin{pmatrix}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},1}^{(5)}-\sqrt{3}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},4}^{(5)}\\
-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},1}^{(5)}-\sqrt{3}Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}-\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},4}^{(5)}\\
\sqrt{2}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},1}^{(5)}-\sqrt{3}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},3}^{(5)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},4}^{(5)}\\
-\sqrt{2}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},1}^{(5)}-\sqrt{3}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},3}^{(5)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},4}^{(5)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

\displaystyle Y_{\mathbf{5}I}^{(6)}

\displaystyle=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{4^{\prime}}}^{(5)})_{\mathbf{5}_{1}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{6}\left(Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}+Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},3}^{(5)}\right)\\
\sqrt{6}Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},1}^{(5)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}+Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},3}^{(5)}-\sqrt{3}Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},4}^{(5)}\\
\sqrt{2}\left(-\sqrt{3}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},1}^{(5)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}+Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},3}^{(5)}\right)\\
\sqrt{2}\left(-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},3}^{(5)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},3}^{(5)}+\sqrt{3}Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},4}^{(5)}\right)\\
-\sqrt{3}Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},1}^{(5)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},2}^{(5)}+\sqrt{2}Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},3}^{(5)}+\sqrt{6}Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{4}^{\prime},4}^{(5)}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

\displaystyle Y_{\mathbf{5}II}^{(6)}

\displaystyle=(Y_{\mathbf{6}}^{(1)}Y_{\mathbf{6}II}^{(5)})_{\mathbf{5}_{2,a}}=\begin{pmatrix}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,1}^{(5)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,2}^{(5)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,3}^{(5)}+2Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,4}^{(5)}-2Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,5}^{(5)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,6}^{(5)}\\
\sqrt{6}\left(Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,3}^{(5)}-Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,1}^{(5)}\right)\\
\sqrt{3}\left(Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,2}^{(5)}-Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,4}^{(5)}+Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,5}^{(5)}-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,6}^{(5)}\right)\\
\sqrt{3}\left(-Y_{5}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,1}^{(5)}-Y_{4}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,3}^{(5)}+Y_{3}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,4}^{(5)}+Y_{1}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,5}^{(5)}\right)\\
\sqrt{6}\left(Y_{2}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,6}^{(5)}-Y_{6}Y_{\mathbf{6}II,2}^{(5)}\right)\end{pmatrix}\,.

We see that the linear space of modular forms of level 5 and weight 6 has dimension 31 which matches with the dimension formula
5k+1=5\times 6+1=31
.

D.1
Higher rational weight modular forms of level 5

In the following, we report the higher rational weight modular forms at level 5, and they are organized into irreducible multiplets of
\widetilde{\Gamma}_{5}
.

Y^{(\frac{6}{5})}_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime 4}}=\frac{1}{2}\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(1)}_{\mathbf{6}^{0}}\right)_{\mathbf{3}^{\prime 4}}=\begin{pmatrix}5\sqrt{2}F_{1}^{3}F_{2}^{3}\\
3F_{1}F_{2}^{5}-F_{1}^{6}\\
F_{2}^{6}+3F_{1}^{5}F_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

Y^{(\frac{6}{5})}_{\mathbf{4}^{4}}=-\frac{1}{3}\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(1)}_{\mathbf{6}^{0}}\right)_{\mathbf{4}^{4}}=\begin{pmatrix}5F_{1}^{2}F_{2}^{4}\\
-F_{1}^{6}-2F_{2}^{5}F_{1}\\
2F_{1}^{5}F_{2}-F_{2}^{6}\\
5F_{1}^{4}F_{2}^{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

Y^{(\frac{7}{5})}_{\mathbf{2^{\prime}}^{3}}=\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(\frac{6}{5})}_{\mathbf{4}^{4}}\right)_{\mathbf{2^{\prime}}^{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}7F_{1}^{5}F_{2}^{2}-F_{2}^{7}\\
-F_{1}^{2}\left(F_{1}^{5}+7F_{2}^{5}\right)\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

Y^{(\frac{7}{5})}_{\mathbf{6}^{3}}=\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(\frac{6}{5})}_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}^{4}}\right)_{\mathbf{6}^{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}-F_{1}\left(F_{2}^{6}+3F_{1}^{5}F_{2}\right)\\
F_{1}F_{2}\left(F_{1}^{5}-3F_{2}^{5}\right)\\
F_{2}^{7}+3F_{1}^{5}F_{2}^{2}\\
-5\sqrt{2}F_{1}^{4}F_{2}^{3}\\
-5\sqrt{2}F_{1}^{3}F_{2}^{4}\\
F_{1}^{7}-3F_{1}^{2}F_{2}^{5}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

Y^{(\frac{8}{5})}_{\mathbf{4}^{2}}=-\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(\frac{7}{5})}_{\mathbf{6}^{3}}\right)_{\mathbf{4}^{2}}=\begin{pmatrix}F_{1}^{3}\left(F_{1}^{5}+7F_{2}^{5}\right)\\
F_{1}^{2}F_{2}\left(F_{1}^{5}+7F_{2}^{5}\right)\\
7F_{1}^{6}F_{2}^{2}-F_{1}F_{2}^{7}\\
F_{2}^{8}-7F_{1}^{5}F_{2}^{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

Y^{(\frac{8}{5})}_{\mathbf{5}^{2}}=-\frac{1}{2}\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(\frac{7}{5})}_{\mathbf{6}^{3}}\right)_{\mathbf{5}^{2}}=\begin{pmatrix}5\sqrt{6}F_{1}^{4}F_{2}^{4}\\

2F_{1}^{2}F_{2}^{6}-4F_{1}^{7}F_{2}\\
2F_{1}F_{2}^{2}\left(F_{1}^{5}+2F_{2}^{5}\right)\\
F_{2}^{8}+8F_{1}^{5}F_{2}^{3}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

Y^{(\frac{9}{5})}_{\mathbf{4^{\prime}}^{1}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(\frac{8}{5})}_{\mathbf{5}^{2}}\right)_{\mathbf{4^{\prime}}^{1}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{3}\left(3F_{1}F_{2}^{8}+4F_{1}^{6}F_{2}^{3}\right)\\
F_{2}^{9}+18F_{1}^{5}F_{2}^{4}\\
18F_{1}^{4}F_{2}^{5}-F_{1}^{9}\\
\sqrt{3}\left(3F_{1}^{8}F_{2}-4F_{1}^{3}F_{2}^{6}\right)\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

Y^{(\frac{9}{5})}_{\mathbf{6}^{1}}=\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(\frac{8}{5})}_{\mathbf{4}^{2}}\right)_{\mathbf{6}^{1}}=\begin{pmatrix}6F_{1}^{7}F_{2}^{2}-8F_{1}^{2}F_{2}^{7}\\
-8F_{2}^{2}F_{1}^{7}-6F_{2}^{7}F_{1}^{2}\\
14F_{1}^{6}F_{2}^{3}-2F_{1}F_{2}^{8}\\
\sqrt{2}F_{2}\left(F_{2}^{8}-7F_{1}^{5}F_{2}^{3}\right)\\
\sqrt{2}F_{1}^{4}\left(F_{1}^{5}+7F_{2}^{5}\right)\\
2F_{1}^{3}F_{2}\left(F_{1}^{5}+7F_{2}^{5}\right)\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

Y^{(2)}_{\mathbf{3}^{0}}=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(\frac{9}{5})}_{\mathbf{4^{\prime}}^{1}}\right)_{\mathbf{3}^{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}F_{1}^{10}-36F_{2}^{5}F_{1}^{5}-F_{2}^{10}\\
5\sqrt{2}F_{1}^{4}F_{2}\left(F_{1}^{5}-3F_{2}^{5}\right)\\
5\sqrt{2}F_{1}F_{2}^{4}\left(3F_{1}^{5}+F_{2}^{5}\right)\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

Y^{(2)}_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}^{0}}=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(\frac{9}{5})}_{\mathbf{6}^{1}}\right)_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}^{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}F_{1}^{10}+14F_{2}^{5}F_{1}^{5}-F_{2}^{10}\\
-5\sqrt{2}F_{1}^{3}F_{2}^{2}\left(F_{1}^{5}+2F_{2}^{5}\right)\\
-5\sqrt{2}\left(2F_{1}^{7}F_{2}^{3}-F_{1}^{2}F_{2}^{8}\right)\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

Y^{(2)}_{\mathbf{5}^{0}}=-\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(\frac{9}{5})}_{\mathbf{6}^{1}}\right)_{\mathbf{5}^{0}}=\begin{pmatrix}-\sqrt{6}\left(F_{1}^{10}+F_{2}^{10}\right)\\
6F_{1}^{4}F_{2}\left(F_{1}^{5}+7F_{2}^{5}\right)\\

-6F_{1}F_{2}^{4}\left(F_{2}^{5}-7F_{1}^{5}\right)\\
\end{pmatrix}\,.

Y_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}^{(\frac{11}{5})}=-\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(2)}_{\mathbf{3}^{0}}\right)_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}=\begin{pmatrix}F_{1}^{11}-66F_{2}^{5}F_{1}^{6}-11F_{2}^{10}F_{1}\\
F_{2}^{11}+66F_{1}^{5}F_{2}^{6}-11F_{1}^{10}F_{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

Y_{\mathbf{4^{\prime}}^{4}}^{(\frac{11}{5})}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(2)}_{\mathbf{3}^{0}}\right)_{\mathbf{4^{\prime}}^{4}}=\begin{pmatrix}-5\sqrt{3}F_{1}^{2}F_{2}^{4}\left(3F_{1}^{5}+F_{2}^{5}\right)\\

5\sqrt{3}F_{1}^{4}F_{2}^{2}\left(F_{1}^{5}-3F_{2}^{5}\right)\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

Y_{\mathbf{6}^{4}}^{(\frac{11}{5})}=\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(2)}_{\mathbf{3^{\prime}}^{0}}\right)_{\mathbf{6}^{4}}=\begin{pmatrix}5\sqrt{2}F_{1}^{3}F_{2}^{3}\left(2F_{1}^{5}-F_{2}^{5}\right)\\

\end{pmatrix}\,,

Y_{\mathbf{1}^{3}}^{(\frac{12}{5})}=\frac{1}{12}\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(\frac{11}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}\right)_{\mathbf{1}^{3}}=F_{1}F_{2}\left(F_{1}^{10}-11F_{2}^{5}F_{1}^{5}-F_{2}^{10}\right)\\
\,,

Y_{\mathbf{3}^{3}}^{(\frac{12}{5})}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(\frac{11}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}\right)_{\mathbf{3}^{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}5\sqrt{2}F_{1}F_{2}\left(F_{1}^{10}+F_{2}^{10}\right)\\

-F_{1}^{12}+66F_{2}^{5}F_{1}^{7}+11F_{2}^{10}F_{1}^{2}\\
\end{pmatrix}\,,

Y_{\mathbf{5}^{3}}^{(\frac{12}{5})}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{3}}\left(Y^{(\frac{1}{5})}_{\mathbf{2}^{4}}Y^{(\frac{11}{5})}_{\mathbf{4^{\prime}}^{4}}\right)_{\mathbf{5}^{3}}=\begin{pmatrix}\sqrt{6}F_{1}F_{2}\left(F_{1}^{10}+14F_{2}^{5}F_{1}^{5}-F_{2}^{10}\right)\\
\left(F_{2}^{6}+3F_{1}^{5}F_{2}\right){}^{2}\\
10F_{1}^{4}F_{2}^{3}\left(F_{1}^{5}-3F_{2}^{5}\right)\\
-10F_{1}^{3}F_{2}^{4}\left(3F_{1}^{5}+F_{2}^{5}\right)\\
\left(F_{1}^{6}-3F_{1}F_{2}^{5}\right){}^{2}\\
